<script>

// Tab Switching System

document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(btn => {

  btn.addEventListener('click', () => {

    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));

    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-pane').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));

    btn.classList.add('active');

    const target = document.getElementById(btn.dataset.tab);

    if(target) target.classList.add('active');

  });

});

// Particle search filter

const particleSearchEl = document.getElementById('particleSearch');

const particlePresetSel = document.getElementById('particlePreset');

const particlePageLabel = document.getElementById('particlePageLabel');

function filterParticleList(){

  if(!particlePresetSel) return;

  const q = (particleSearchEl.value || '').trim().toLowerCase();

  let shown = 0, first = -1;

  Array.from(particlePresetSel.options).forEach((o, i) => {

    const ok = !q || o.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q);

    o.hidden = !ok;

    if(ok){

      shown++;

      if(first < 0) first = i;

    }

  });

  if(q && first >= 0){

    particlePresetSel.selectedIndex = first;

    particlePresetSel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', {bubbles: true}));

  }

  if(particlePageLabel) {

    particlePageLabel.textContent = (particlePresetSel.selectedIndex + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0') + ' / 300';

  }

}

particleSearchEl?.addEventListener('input', filterParticleList);

particlePresetSel?.addEventListener('change', () => {

  if(particlePageLabel) {

    particlePageLabel.textContent = (particlePresetSel.selectedIndex + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0') + ' / 300';

  }

});

const $=id=>document.getElementById(id),C=$('canvas'),X=C.getContext('2d'),A=$('audio'),F=$('file'),S=$('start'),M=$('mode');

const ids=['sens','smooth','fft','bars','glow','decay','minf','maxf','rotation'],fv=[512,1024,2048,4096,8192,16384];

let ac=null,src=null,an=null,fd=null,wd=null,url=null,raf=0,started=false,peaks=[],hue=190,parts=[];

function resize(){let d=devicePixelRatio||1,r=C.getBoundingClientRect();C.width=r.width*d;C.height=r.height*d;X.setTransform(d,0,0,d,0,0);if(!parts.length)for(let i=0;i<90;i++)parts.push({x:Math.random(),y:Math.random(),v:.0002+Math.random()*.0006,s:.5+Math.random()*2})}addEventListener('resize',resize);resize();

function lab(){$('sensO').textContent=(+$('sens').value).toFixed(2)+'×';$('smoothO').textContent=(+$('smooth').value).toFixed(2);$('fftO').textContent=fv[+$('fft').value];$('barsO').textContent=$('bars').value;$('glowO').textContent=$('glow').value;$('decayO').textContent=(+$('decay').value).toFixed(2);$('minO').textContent=$('minf').value+' Hz';$('maxO').textContent=(+$('maxf').value/1000).toFixed(1)+' kHz';$('rotO').textContent=$('rotation').value+'°'}lab();

F.onchange=async()=>{let f=F.files[0];if(!f)return;if(url)URL.revokeObjectURL(url);url=URL.createObjectURL(f);A.src=url;$('track').textContent=f.name;S.disabled=false;started=false;try{await init();await A.play()}catch(e){statusText('START tugmasini bosing: '+e.message)}};

ids.forEach(id=>$(id).addEventListener('input',()=>{lab();if(an){an.smoothingTimeConstant=+$('smooth').value;if(id==='fft'){an.fftSize=fv[+$('fft').value];fd=new Uint8Array(an.frequencyBinCount);wd=new Uint8Array(an.fftSize)}}}));

// DISPLAY OPTIONS: CHANGE COLOR + RESET

$('color').addEventListener('click', ()=>{

  hue = (hue + 45) % 360;

  parts.forEach((p,i)=>{

    p.x = (p.x + 0.013 + (i%7)*0.001) % 1;

    p.y = (p.y + 0.009 + (i%5)*0.001) % 1;

  });

  draw();

});

$('reset').addEventListener('click', ()=>{

  const defaults = {

    sens:'1.5', smooth:'.75', fft:'3', bars:'96', glow:'22',

    decay:'.88', minf:'30', maxf:'16000', rotation:'0'

  };

  Object.entries(defaults).forEach(([id,val])=>$(id).value=val);

  ['grid','labels','reflection'].forEach(id=>$(id).checked=false);

  $('peaks').checked=true;

  $('stars').checked=true;

  $('beat').checked=true;

  hue = 190;

  peaks = [];

  parts = [];

  resize();

  lab();

  if(an){

    an.smoothingTimeConstant = .75;

    an.fftSize = fv[3];

    fd = new Uint8Array(an.frequencyBinCount);

    wd = new Uint8Array(an.fftSize);

  }

  draw();

  statusText('Visualizer sozlamalari reset qilindi.');

});

async function init(){if(!F.files[0])return;if(!ac)ac=new(window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();if(ac.state==='suspended')await ac.resume();if(!src){src=ac.createMediaElementSource(A);an=ac.createAnalyser();an.fftSize=fv[+$('fft').value];an.smoothingTimeConstant=+$('smooth').value;src.connect(an);an.connect(ac.destination);fd=new Uint8Array(an.frequencyBinCount);wd=new Uint8Array(an.fftSize)}$('sr').textContent=ac.sampleRate+' Hz';$('fft2').textContent=an.fftSize;started=true;draw();}S.onclick=async()=>{try{await init();if(A.paused)await A.play()}catch(e){statusText('START xatosi: '+e.message)}};A.onplay=()=>{started=true;if(!raf)draw()};A.onpause=()=>{};A.onended=()=>{};

function ci(h){return Math.max(0,Math.min(fd.length-1,Math.round(h/(ac.sampleRate/2)*(fd.length-1))))}function av(a,b){let s=0,n=0;for(let i=a;i<=b;i++){s+=fd[i];n++}return n?s/n:0}function clr(w,h){X.fillStyle='#01040a';X.fillRect(0,0,w,h)}function color(t){return`hsl(${hue+120*t},100%,${55+14*t}%)`}

function grid(w,h){ /* Frequency grid and Hz labels intentionally disabled. */ }

function drawExtraMode(w,h,mode){

  if(!fd || !fd.length) return;

  if(mode==='ring'){

    let cx=w/2, cy=h/2, base=Math.min(w,h)*.18, n=Math.min(180,fd.length);

    X.save(); X.translate(cx,cy); X.shadowBlur=+$('glow').value;

    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){

      let v=Math.min(1,fd[i]/255*+$('sens').value), a=i/n*Math.PI*2, r=base+v*Math.min(w,h)*.24;

      X.strokeStyle=color(i/n); X.shadowColor=X.strokeStyle; X.lineWidth=2;

      X.beginPath(); X.arc(0,0,r,a,a+.018); X.stroke();

    }

    X.restore();

  } else if(mode==='tunnel'){

    let cx=w/2,cy=h/2,n=24;

    X.save();X.translate(cx,cy);

    for(let k=0;k<n;k++){

      let idx=Math.min(fd.length-1,Math.floor(k/n*fd.length)),v=fd[idx]/255;

      let r=(k/n)*Math.min(w,h)*.48 + v*25;

      X.strokeStyle=color(k/n);X.globalAlpha=.25+.7*(1-k/n);X.lineWidth=1+v*3;

      X.beginPath();X.arc(0,0,r,0,Math.PI*2);X.stroke();

    }

    X.restore();

  } else if(mode==='orbit'){

    let cx=w/2,cy=h/2,n=80;

    X.save();X.shadowBlur=+$('glow').value;

    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){

      let v=fd[Math.floor(i/n*fd.length)]/255, a=i/n*Math.PI*2+performance.now()/1800;

      let r=Math.min(w,h)*(.12+.24*v),x=cx+Math.cos(a)*r,y=cy+Math.sin(a)*r;

      X.fillStyle=color(i/n);X.shadowColor=X.fillStyle;X.beginPath();X.arc(x,y,1.5+v*5,0,Math.PI*2);X.fill();

    }

    X.restore();

  } else if(mode==='matrix'){

    X.save();X.font='12px monospace';

    for(let x=0;x<w;x+=16){

      let idx=Math.floor((x/w)*fd.length),v=fd[idx]/255;

      let len=Math.floor(v*14);

      for(let j=0;j<len;j++){X.fillStyle=`hsla(${hue},100%,${45+j*3}%,${.12+j/len*.8})`;X.fillText(Math.random()>.5?'0':'1',x,(j+1)*24)}

    }

    X.restore();

  }

}

function draw(){

  cancelAnimationFrame(raf);

  const loop=()=>{

    const w=C.clientWidth,h=C.clientHeight;

    if(!w||!h){raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);return}

    X.clearRect(0,0,w,h);

    clr(w,h);

    drawBackground(w,h);

    drawBackgroundParticles(w,h);

    if(!an||!fd){

      drawTextOverlay(w,h);

      raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);

      return;

    }

    an.getByteFrequencyData(fd);

    an.getByteTimeDomainData(wd);

    const selectedMode=M.value;

    const baseModes=['bars','mirror','wave','circle','radial','dots','ring','tunnel','orbit','matrix'];

    let visualVariant=0;

    let mode=selectedMode;

    if(selectedMode.startsWith('v')){

      const n=parseInt(selectedMode.slice(1),10)||0;

      mode=baseModes[n%baseModes.length];

      visualVariant=Math.floor(n/baseModes.length)+1;

    }

    const variantScale=visualVariant ? (0.82 + (visualVariant%8)*0.055) : 1;

    const bars=Math.max(16,Math.min(180,Math.round((+$('bars').value||64)*variantScale)));

    const sens=Math.max(.1,(+$('sens').value||1)*(visualVariant ? (0.88+(visualVariant%7)*0.07) : 1));

    const glow=Math.max(0,(+$('glow').value||12)+(visualVariant ? (visualVariant%10)*2 : 0));

    const decay=Math.max(.01,Math.min(.99,+$('decay').value||.8));

    const rot=((+$('rotation').value||0)+(visualVariant ? (visualVariant*13)%360 : 0))*Math.PI/180;

    X.save();

    X.translate(w/2,h/2);

    X.rotate(rot);

    X.translate(-w/2,-h/2);

    X.shadowBlur=glow;

    if(visualVariant){

      X.globalAlpha=0.86 + (visualVariant%5)*0.035;

    }

    if(mode==='bars'||mode==='mirror'){

      const bw=w/bars;

      for(let i=0;i<bars;i++){

        const idx=Math.min(fd.length-1,Math.floor(i/bars*fd.length));

        const v=Math.min(1,(fd[idx]/255)*sens);

        const bh=Math.max(2,v*h*.42);

        X.fillStyle=color(i/Math.max(1,bars-1));

        X.shadowColor=X.fillStyle;

        if(mode==='bars'){

          X.fillRect(i*bw,h*.5-bh,bw*.72,bh);

        }else{

          X.fillRect(i*bw,h*.5-bh/2,bw*.72,bh/2);

          X.fillRect(i*bw,h*.5,bw*.72,bh/2);

        }

      }

    }else if(mode==='wave'){

      X.shadowColor=color(.5);X.strokeStyle=color(.5);X.lineWidth=2.5;

      X.beginPath();

      const step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(wd.length/w));

      for(let x=0;x<w;x++){

        const v=(wd[Math.min(wd.length-1,x*step)]-128)/128;

        const y=h/2+v*h*.32;

        if(x===0)X.moveTo(x,y);else X.lineTo(x,y);

      }

      X.stroke();

    }else if(mode==='circle'||mode==='radial'){

      const cx=w/2,cy=h/2,base=Math.min(w,h)*.17,n=Math.min(bars,fd.length);

      X.translate(cx,cy);

      for(let i=0;i<n;i++){

        const v=Math.min(1,(fd[i]/255)*sens);

        const a=i/n*Math.PI*2-Math.PI/2;

        const inner=base;

        const outer=base+v*Math.min(w,h)*(mode==='radial'?.34:.25);

        X.strokeStyle=color(i/n);X.shadowColor=X.strokeStyle;

        X.lineWidth=Math.max(1,mode==='radial'?3:2);

        X.beginPath();X.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*inner,Math.sin(a)*inner);

        X.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*outer,Math.sin(a)*outer);X.stroke();

      }

      X.beginPath();X.arc(0,0,base,0,Math.PI*2);

      X.strokeStyle=color(.5);X.lineWidth=2;X.stroke();

      X.translate(-cx,-cy);

    }else if(mode==='dots'){

      const cx=w/2,cy=h/2,n=Math.min(140,fd.length);

      for(let i=0;i<n;i++){

        const v=Math.min(1,(fd[i]/255)*sens),a=i/n*Math.PI*2;

        const r=Math.min(w,h)*(.13+.34*v);

        const x=cx+Math.cos(a)*r,y=cy+Math.sin(a)*r;

        X.fillStyle=color(i/n);X.shadowColor=X.fillStyle;

        X.beginPath();X.arc(x,y,1.5+v*5,0,Math.PI*2);X.fill();

      }

    }

    X.restore();

    drawExtraMode(w,h,mode);

    drawEffectLayer(w,h);

    drawTextOverlay(w,h);

    if($('peak'))$('peak').textContent='PEAK '+(Math.max(...fd)/2.55-100).toFixed(1)+' dB';

    raf=requestAnimationFrame(loop);

  };

  loop();

}

let mediaRecorder=null;

let recordStream=null;

let recordAudioDest=null;

let recordedChunks=[];

let exportAnimationId=0;

let exporting=false;

async function download1080Video(){

  if(exporting) return;

  const resetExportUI=()=>{

    exporting=false;

    cancelAnimationFrame(exportAnimationId);

    $('download1080').disabled=false;

    $('download1080').textContent='⬇ DOWNLOAD 1080P';

  };

  try{

    try{await ensureFontLoaded(TE.textFont.value||'Arial');}catch(e){}

    if(!F.files[0]){

      alert('Avval audio fayl tanlang.');

      return;

    }

    if(!ac || !an) await init();

    if(!ac || !an || !src) throw new Error('Audio engine ishga tushmadi.');

    await ac.resume();

    const outW=1920, outH=1080;

    const recCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');

    recCanvas.width=outW;

    recCanvas.height=outH;

    const rc=recCanvas.getContext('2d',{alpha:false});

    rc.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;

    rc.imageSmoothingQuality='high';

    exporting=true;

    $('download1080').disabled=true;

    $('download1080').textContent='⏳ CREATING H.264...';

    statusText('H.264 High 1080p 60 FPS eksport qilinmoqda...');

    const paint=()=>{

      if(!exporting) return;

      rc.fillStyle='#01040a';

      rc.fillRect(0,0,outW,outH);

      const sw=C.width || C.clientWidth;

      const sh=C.height || C.clientHeight;

      const scale=Math.min(outW/sw,outH/sh);

      const dw=sw*scale, dh=sh*scale;

      const dx=(outW-dw)/2, dy=(outH-dh)/2;

      rc.drawImage(C,0,0,sw,sh,dx,dy,dw,dh);

      exportAnimationId=requestAnimationFrame(paint);

    };

    paint();

    const fps=60;

    const videoStream=recCanvas.captureStream(fps);

    recordAudioDest=ac.createMediaStreamDestination();

    src.connect(recordAudioDest);

    const audioTracks=recordAudioDest.stream.getAudioTracks();

    const videoTracks=videoStream.getVideoTracks();

    if(!audioTracks.length) throw new Error('Audio track yaratilmadi.');

    recordStream=new MediaStream([...videoTracks,...audioTracks]);

    const mimeCandidates=[

      'video/mp4;codecs="avc1.64002A,mp4a.40.2"',

      'video/mp4;codecs="avc1.640028,mp4a.40.2"',

      'video/mp4;codecs="avc1.4D002A,mp4a.40.2"',

      'video/mp4'

    ];

    const mime=mimeCandidates.find(x=>window.MediaRecorder && MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(x));

    if(!mime){

      throw new Error('Bu brauzer H.264/AAC MP4 eksportini qo‘llab-quvvatlamaydi. Chrome/Edge yangilangan versiyasidan foydalaning.');

    }

    recordedChunks=[];

    mediaRecorder=new MediaRecorder(recordStream,{

      mimeType:mime,

      videoBitsPerSecond:100000000,

      audioBitsPerSecond:320000

    });

    let finished=false;

    let finishTimer=null;

    const cleanupTracks=()=>{

      try{recordStream?.getTracks().forEach(tr=>tr.stop());}catch(e){}

      try{src.disconnect(recordAudioDest);}catch(e){}

      recordAudioDest=null;

      recordStream=null;

    };

    const finish=()=>{

      if(finished) return;

      finished=true;

      if(finishTimer) clearTimeout(finishTimer);

      finishTimer=setTimeout(()=>{

        if(mediaRecorder && mediaRecorder.state!=='inactive'){

          mediaRecorder.stop();

        }

      },120);

    };

    mediaRecorder.ondataavailable=e=>{

      if(e.data && e.data.size>0) recordedChunks.push(e.data);

    };

    mediaRecorder.onstop=()=>{

      cancelAnimationFrame(exportAnimationId);

      cleanupTracks();

      if(!recordedChunks.length){

        resetExportUI();

        statusText('Video yaratilmadi. Qaytadan urinib ko‘ring.');

        return;

      }

      const blob=new Blob(recordedChunks,{type:mime});

      const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);

      const baseName=(F.files[0]?.name||'audio-spectrum').replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'');

      const a=document.createElement('a');

      a.href=url;

      a.download=baseName+'-1080p-H264-High.mp4';

      a.style.display='none';

      document.body.appendChild(a);

      a.click();

      setTimeout(()=>{

        a.remove();

        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      },15000);

      resetExportUI();

      statusText('H.264 High 1080p 60 FPS MP4 tayyor — audio bilan yuklab olindi.');

    };

    mediaRecorder.onerror=e=>{

      if(finishTimer) clearTimeout(finishTimer);

      cleanupTracks();

      resetExportUI();

      statusText('Export xatosi: '+(e.error?.message||'unknown error'));

    };

    A.loop=false;

    A.onended=finish;

    A.pause();

    A.currentTime=0;

    mediaRecorder.start(1000);

    try{

      await A.play();

    }catch(playError){

      if(mediaRecorder && mediaRecorder.state!=='inactive'){

        try{mediaRecorder.stop();}catch(e){}

      }

      throw new Error('Musiqani eksport uchun ishga tushirib bo‘lmadi: '+playError.message);

    }

  }catch(e){

    resetExportUI();

    statusText('Export xatosi: '+e.message);

    alert(e.message);

  }

}

function statusText(t){

  const h=document.querySelector('.hint');

  if(h) h.textContent=t;

}

const SYSTEM_FONT_FAMILIES=["Arial", "Arial Black", "Calibri", "Cambria", "Candara", "Century Gothic", "Consolas", "Constantia", "Corbel", "Courier New", "Franklin Gothic Medium", "Garamond", "Georgia", "Impact", "Lucida Console", "Lucida Sans Unicode", "Segoe UI", "Tahoma", "Times New Roman", "Trebuchet MS", "Verdana", "Yu Gothic", "Meiryo", "Malgun Gothic", "Leelawadee UI", "Nirmala UI", "Bahnschrift", "Aptos", "Aptos Display", "Rockwell"];

const FONT_VARIANTS=['REGULAR','MEDIUM','SEMIBOLD','BOLD','BLACK'];

const FONT_CATALOG=[];

GOOGLE_FONT_FAMILIES.forEach(f=>FONT_VARIANTS.forEach((variant,i)=>FONT_CATALOG.push({family:f,variant,weight:[400,500,600,700,900][i],source:'google'})));

SYSTEM_FONT_FAMILIES.forEach(f=>FONT_CATALOG.push({family:f,variant:'SYSTEM',weight:400,source:'system'}));

const fontCache=new Set();

const fontLinks=new Map();

function ensureFontLoaded(family){

  if(!family || fontCache.has(family)) return Promise.resolve();

  if(!fontLinks.has(family)){

    const link=document.createElement('link');

    link.rel='stylesheet';

    link.href='https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family='+encodeURIComponent(family).replace(/%20/g,'+')+'&display=swap';

    link.dataset.itShakhFont=family;

    document.head.appendChild(link);

    fontLinks.set(family,link);

  }

  return new Promise(resolve=>{

    let done=false;

    const finish=()=>{if(done)return;done=true;fontCache.add(family);resolve();};

    if(document.fonts && document.fonts.load){

      Promise.race([

        document.fonts.load('400 40px "'+family+'"'),

        new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,5000))

      ]).then(finish).catch(finish);

    }else setTimeout(finish,1200);

  });

}

function populateFontCatalog(){

  const sel=TE.textFont;

  if(!sel)return;

  sel.innerHTML='';

  const frag=document.createDocumentFragment();

  FONT_CATALOG.forEach((f,i)=>{

    const o=document.createElement('option');

    o.value=f.family;

    o.dataset.weight=f.weight;

    o.dataset.source=f.source;

    o.textContent=String(i+1).padStart(4,'0')+' • '+f.family+' • '+f.variant;

    frag.appendChild(o);

  });

  sel.appendChild(frag);

  sel.value='Arial';

}

populateFontCatalog();

TE.textFont.addEventListener('change',async()=>{

  await ensureFontLoaded(TE.textFont.value);

  try{await document.fonts.load('400 '+TE.textSize.value+'px "'+TE.textFont.value+'"')}catch(e){}

  draw();

});

function textLabels(){

 $('textSizeO').textContent=TE.textSize.value+'px';$('letterO').textContent=TE.letter.value+'px';

 $('textOpacityO').textContent=TE.textOpacity.value+'%';$('outlineO').textContent=TE.outline.value+'px';

 $('textGlowO').textContent=TE.textGlow.value;$('animSpeedO').textContent=(+TE.animSpeed.value).toFixed(1)+'×';

 $('textXO').textContent=TE.textX.value+'%';$('textYO').textContent=TE.textY.value+'%';

 $('textRotO').textContent=TE.textRot.value+'°';$('textScaleO').textContent=(+TE.textScale.value).toFixed(2)+'×';

}

TIDS.forEach(id=>TE[id].addEventListener('input',textLabels));textLabels();

function applyTextPreset(v){

 if(/^tp\d+$/.test(v)){

   const n=Number(v.replace(/^tp/,'')), themeIndex=Math.floor(n/100), styleIndex=Math.floor((n%100)/10), animIndex=n%10;

   const themes=[

    ["IT SHAKH","#00e5ff","#7c4dff","gradient"],["CYBER SYSTEM","#00ff99","#00e5ff","neon"],["FIRE BASS","#ff3b2f","#ffd45c","gradient"],

    ["ICE WAVE","#8ee8ff","#ffffff","gradient"],["GALAXY SOUND","#a66cff","#ff4fd8","gradient"],["MATRIX NODE","#35ef9b","#00ff66","neon"],

    ["GOLDEN AUDIO","#ffd45c","#ff9f43","gradient"],["VIOLET PULSE","#b36bff","#7c4dff","neon"],["PINK ENERGY","#ff4fd8","#ff7abf","gradient"],

    ["AQUA DREAM","#38f0d0","#00e5ff","gradient"],["LASER SOUND","#ff3b5c","#ff7b91","neon"],["SUNSET MIX","#ff7a3d","#ffd45c","gradient"],

    ["OCEAN DEEP","#208cff","#38f0d0","gradient"],["LIME BOOST","#a7ff3d","#35ef9b","neon"],["ELECTRIC MIX","#f5ff3d","#00e5ff","rainbow"],

    ["ROYAL AUDIO","#7c4dff","#d9a7ff","gradient"],["MAGENTA CLUB","#ff19d1","#7c4dff","neon"],["PLASMA CORE","#ff4fa3","#00e5ff","rainbow"],

    ["STEEL WAVE","#b9d4e8","#6b8ba4","glass"],["DARK CINEMA","#ffffff","#708090","glass"]

   ];

   const styles=[

    ["HUGE TITLE","900",72,4,35],["CINEMA","700",64,2,18],["CLUB","900",54,3,40],["MINIMAL","700",42,1,8],

    ["GAMING","900",58,5,32],["TECH","900",48,4,30],["SUBTITLE","600",34,1,10],["RETRO","900",60,5,20],

    ["GLASS","700",50,2,25],["ULTRA","900",82,6,45]

   ];

   const anims=["none","pulse","float","bounce","wave","glitch","type","zoom","spin","shake"];

   const th=themes[themeIndex], st=styles[styleIndex], anim=anims[animIndex];

   const set=(id,val)=>{TE[id].value=val};

   set("mainText",th[0]); set("subText",["AUDIO SPECTRUM","VISUAL MIX","BASS / LIGHT / ENERGY","IN MOTION"][styleIndex%4]);

   set("textFont",["Segoe UI","Arial","Impact","Georgia","Courier New","Trebuchet MS"][themeIndex%6]);

   set("textWeight",st[1]); set("textSize",st[2]+((animIndex%3)*4)); set("letter",(themeIndex%5)+1);

   set("textOpacity",90+(animIndex%3)*5); set("outline",st[3]); set("textGlow",st[4]+(animIndex%4)*3);

   set("animSpeed",(0.7+(animIndex%7)*0.25).toFixed(2)); set("textX",35+((themeIndex*17+styleIndex*7)%31));

   set("textY",38+((styleIndex*9+animIndex*3)%25)); set("textRot",-12+((themeIndex*13+styleIndex*5+animIndex)%25));

   set("textScale",(0.85+(styleIndex%5)*0.1).toFixed(2)); set("textColor",th[1]); set("textColor2",th[2]);

   set("outlineColor","#02050a"); set("textEffect",th[3]); set("textAnim",anim); textLabels();

   return;

 }

}

$('textPreset').addEventListener('change',e=>applyTextPreset(e.target.value));

function drawTextOverlay(w,h){

 if(!TE.textEnabled.checked)return;

 const main=TE.mainText.value||'', sub=TE.subText.value||''; if(!main&&!sub)return;

 const now=performance.now()/1000, speed=+TE.animSpeed.value, anim=TE.textAnim.value;

 let x=w*(+TE.textX.value/100), y=h*(+TE.textY.value/100), scale=+TE.textScale.value, rot=(+TE.textRot.value)*Math.PI/180;

 let alpha=+TE.textOpacity.value/100;

 if(anim==='pulse')scale*=1+.045*Math.sin(now*5*speed);

 if(anim==='float')y+=Math.sin(now*2*speed)*10;

 if(anim==='bounce')y+=Math.abs(Math.sin(now*3*speed))*14;

 if(anim==='wave')rot+=Math.sin(now*2*speed)*.035;

 if(anim==='spin')rot+=now*speed*.35;

 if(anim==='zoom')scale*=.9+.12*(.5+.5*Math.sin(now*2*speed));

 X.save();X.translate(x,y);X.rotate(rot);X.scale(scale,scale);X.globalAlpha=alpha;

 X.textAlign=TE.textAlign.value.toLowerCase();X.textBaseline='middle';

 const fontFamily=TE.textFont.value||'Arial';

 X.font=TE.textWeight.value+' '+TE.textSize.value+'px "'+fontFamily+'", sans-serif';

 X.shadowBlur=+TE.textGlow.value;X.shadowColor=TE.textColor.value;

 let effect=TE.textEffect.value, fill=TE.textColor.value;

 if(effect==='gradient'){

   const width=Math.max(200,Math.abs(X.measureText(main).width)); const g=X.createLinearGradient(-width/2,0,width/2,0);g.addColorStop(0,TE.textColor.value);g.addColorStop(1,TE.textColor2.value);fill=g;

 }else if(effect==='rainbow'){

   const g=X.createLinearGradient(-300,0,300,0);g.addColorStop(0,TE.textColor.value);g.addColorStop(.5,TE.textColor2.value);g.addColorStop(1,TE.textColor.value);fill=g;

 }else if(effect==='glass'){fill='rgba(255,255,255,.72)'}

 X.fillStyle=fill;X.strokeStyle=TE.outlineColor.value;X.lineWidth=+TE.outline.value;X.lineJoin='round';

 function drawLine(txt,yy){

   if(!txt)return;

   if(anim==='glitch'){X.globalAlpha=alpha*.35;X.fillStyle='#ff2e7d';X.fillText(txt,2,yy);X.fillStyle='#00e5ff';X.fillText(txt,-2,yy);X.globalAlpha=alpha}

   if(+TE.outline.value)X.strokeText(txt,0,yy);

   X.fillText(txt,0,yy);

 }

 drawLine(main, sub ? -TE.textSize.value*.34 : 0);

 if(sub){X.font=Math.round(+TE.textSize.value*.38)+'px "'+(TE.textFont.value||'Arial')+'", sans-serif';X.globalAlpha=alpha*.86;drawLine(sub,+TE.textSize.value*.65)}

 X.restore();

}

// 4000 EFFECT PRESETS

const effectPresetEl=document.getElementById('effectPreset');

let effectStyle='none',effectStrength=1,effectSpeed=1,effectVariant=0;

function applyEffectPreset(v){

  if(!v)return;

  const n=Math.max(0,Math.min(3999,Number(String(v).replace(/^e/,'')||0)));

  const family=Math.floor(n/200);

  const intensity=Math.floor((n%200)/20);

  const variant=n%20;

  effectVariant=variant;

  effectStrength=.35+intensity*.18;

  effectSpeed=.55+variant*.08;

  const styles=['glow','bass','trail','chromatic','shockwave','aurora','stars','vortex','ring','rain','glitch','scan','rays','burst','bloom','strobe','echo','dust','shift','flare'];

  effectStyle=styles[family];

  $('glow').value=Math.min(45,Math.round(5+effectStrength*18+variant*.6));

  $('decay').value=Math.min(.99,(.62+effectStrength*.035).toFixed(2));

  $('sens').value=Math.min(3.5,(.75+effectStrength*.12).toFixed(2));

  $('smooth').value=Math.min(.95,(.52+variant*.018).toFixed(2));

  $('rotation').value=(family*17+variant*23)%361;

  if($('stars'))$('stars').checked=['stars','burst','dust','aurora','vortex'].includes(effectStyle)||variant%3===0;

  if($('reflection'))$('reflection').checked=variant%4===0||['echo','vortex','ring'].includes(effectStyle);

  if($('beat'))$('beat').checked=['bass','shockwave','burst','strobe','flare'].includes(effectStyle)||variant%5===0;

  lab();

}

effectPresetEl.addEventListener('change',e=>applyEffectPreset(e.target.value));

function drawEffectLayer(w,h){

  if(!effectStyle||effectStyle==='none'||!fd||!fd.length)return;

  const tNow=performance.now()/1000;

  const strength=Math.min(2.5,effectStrength);

  const cx=w/2,cy=h/2;

  X.save();

  if(effectStyle==='glow'||effectStyle==='bloom'){

    X.fillStyle=`hsla(${hue},100%,60%,${Math.min(.08,.018*strength)})`;

    X.fillRect(0,0,w,h);

  }else if(effectStyle==='bass'||effectStyle==='shockwave'){

    const b=av(ci(30),ci(180))/255;

    if(b>.15){

      const r=Math.min(w,h)*(.12+b*.38+(effectStyle==='shockwave'?(tNow*strength*80)%80:0));

      X.strokeStyle=`hsla(${hue},100%,65%,${Math.min(.55,b*strength)})`;

      X.lineWidth=2+b*5;X.shadowBlur=+$('glow').value;X.shadowColor=X.strokeStyle;

      X.beginPath();X.arc(cx,cy,r,0,Math.PI*2);X.stroke();

    }

  }else if(effectStyle==='trail'||effectStyle==='echo'){

    X.globalAlpha=.12*strength;

    for(let k=1;k<6;k++){

      X.strokeStyle=color((k/6+tNow*.03)%1);

      X.lineWidth=1+k*.35;

      X.beginPath();X.arc(cx,cy,Math.min(w,h)*(.14+k*.045),0,Math.PI*2);X.stroke();

    }

  }else if(effectStyle==='chromatic'||effectStyle==='shift'||effectStyle==='flare'){

    X.globalCompositeOperation='screen';

    X.fillStyle=`hsla(${(hue+35)%360},100%,55%,${.025*strength})`;X.fillRect(0,0,w,h);

  }else if(effectStyle==='aurora'){

    const g=X.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,0,cx,cy,Math.max(w,h)*.7);

    g.addColorStop(0,`hsla(${hue},100%,65%,${.08*strength})`);

    g.addColorStop(.55,`hsla(${(hue+90)%360},100%,55%,${.035*strength})`);

    g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');

    X.fillStyle=g;X.fillRect(0,0,w,h);

  }else if(effectStyle==='stars'||effectStyle==='dust'||effectStyle==='burst'){

    const n=effectStyle==='burst'?130:75;

    X.shadowBlur=+$('glow').value;

    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){

      const a=i/n*Math.PI*2+tNow*(effectStyle==='burst'?effectSpeed*.8:.08);

      const v=fd[Math.floor(i/n*fd.length)]/255;

      const r=Math.min(w,h)*(.1+v*.4)+(i%7)*9;

      const x=cx+Math.cos(a)*r,y=cy+Math.sin(a)*r;

      X.fillStyle=color((i/n+tNow*.02)%1);

      X.beginPath();X.arc(x,y,1+v*3,0,Math.PI*2);X.fill();

    }

  }else if(effectStyle==='vortex'||effectStyle==='ring'){

    X.globalAlpha=.22*strength;

    X.lineWidth=1.5;

    for(let k=0;k<14;k++){

      const r=Math.min(w,h)*(.08+k*.025);

      X.strokeStyle=color((k/14+tNow*.05)%1);

      X.beginPath();X.arc(cx,cy,r,tNow*.4+k*.2,tNow*.4+k*.2+Math.PI*1.5);X.stroke();

    }

  }else if(effectStyle==='rain'){

    X.font='12px monospace';

    for(let x=0;x<w;x+=18){

      const v=fd[Math.floor((x/w)*fd.length)]/255;

      for(let j=0;j<Math.floor(v*12);j++){

        X.fillStyle=`hsla(${hue},100%,65%,${.08+j*.05})`;

        X.fillText(Math.random()>.5?'0':'1',x,(j+1)*25);

      }

    }

  }else if(effectStyle==='glitch'){

    X.globalAlpha=.16*strength;

    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){

      const y=Math.random()*h;

      X.fillStyle=color(Math.random());

      X.fillRect(0,y,w,1+Math.random()*4);

    }

  }else if(effectStyle==='scan'){

    const y=(tNow*effectSpeed*120)%h;

    X.fillStyle=`hsla(${hue},100%,70%,${.18*strength})`;

    X.fillRect(0,y,w,2);

  }else if(effectStyle==='rays'){

    X.translate(cx,cy);X.globalAlpha=.12*strength;

    X.strokeStyle=color(.2);X.lineWidth=1;

    const n=18;

    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=i/n*Math.PI*2+tNow*.1;X.beginPath();X.moveTo(0,0);X.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*w,Math.sin(a)*h);X.stroke()}

  }else if(effectStyle==='strobe'){

    const b=av(ci(40),ci(160))/255;

    if(b>.62){X.fillStyle=`rgba(255,255,255,${Math.min(.08,(b-.62)*.35*strength)})`;X.fillRect(0,0,w,h)}

  }

  X.restore();

}

// FULLSCREEN

const visualStage=document.getElementById('visual');

const topFullscreen=document.getElementById('fullscreen');

const smallFullscreen=document.getElementById('fs');

let fsFallback=false;

function fallbackFullscreen(on){

  fsFallback=on;

  if(!visualStage)return;

  if(on){

    document.body.style.overflow='hidden';

    visualStage.dataset.oldStyle=visualStage.getAttribute('style')||'';

    Object.assign(visualStage.style,{

      position:'fixed',left:'0',top:'0',width:'100vw',height:'100vh',

      margin:'0',zIndex:'999999',borderRadius:'0'

    });

    visualStage.classList.add('it-fs-stage');

  }else{

    document.body.style.overflow='';

    const old=visualStage.dataset.oldStyle;

    if(old!==undefined)visualStage.setAttribute('style',old);

    else visualStage.removeAttribute('style');

    visualStage.classList.remove('it-fs-stage');

  }

  setTimeout(resize,80);

}

async function toggleVisualFullscreen(){

  if(document.fullscreenElement){

    try{await document.exitFullscreen();}catch(e){}

    return;

  }

  try{

    if(visualStage && visualStage.requestFullscreen){

      await visualStage.requestFullscreen({navigationUI:'hide'});

      return;

    }

  }catch(e){}

  fallbackFullscreen(!fsFallback);

}

if(topFullscreen)topFullscreen.addEventListener('click',toggleVisualFullscreen);

if(smallFullscreen)smallFullscreen.addEventListener('click',toggleVisualFullscreen);

document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange',()=>{

  const nativeOn=!!document.fullscreenElement;

  document.body.classList.toggle('it-fs-native',nativeOn);

  document.documentElement.classList.toggle('it-fs-html',nativeOn);

  document.body.classList.toggle('it-fs-body',nativeOn);

  if(nativeOn){

    if(visualStage){

      visualStage.dataset.nativeOldStyle=visualStage.getAttribute('style')||'';

      visualStage.classList.add('it-fs-stage');

      Object.assign(visualStage.style,{

        width:'100vw',height:'100vh',minWidth:'100vw',minHeight:'100vh',

        maxWidth:'none',maxHeight:'none',position:'fixed',left:'0',top:'0'

      });

    }

    setTimeout(()=>{resize();},80);

    setTimeout(()=>{resize();},250);

  }else{

    if(visualStage){

      visualStage.classList.remove('it-fs-stage');

      const oldNative=visualStage.dataset.nativeOldStyle;

      if(oldNative!==undefined){

        if(oldNative) visualStage.setAttribute('style',oldNative);

        else visualStage.removeAttribute('style');

        delete visualStage.dataset.nativeOldStyle;

      }

    }

    document.documentElement.classList.remove('it-fs-html');

    document.body.classList.remove('it-fs-body');

    if(fsFallback)fallbackFullscreen(false);

    setTimeout(resize,80);

    setTimeout(resize,250);

  }

});

document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{

  if(e.key==='Escape' && fsFallback)fallbackFullscreen(false);

});

// BACKGROUND IMAGE STUDIO

var bgImage=null,bgImageURL='',bgEnergySmooth=0;

function bg$(id){return document.getElementById(id)}

function bgNum(id,def){

  const el=bg$(id); const n=el?parseFloat(el.value):def;

  return Number.isFinite(n)?n:def;

}

function bgOutput(id,val){const el=bg$(id);if(el)el.textContent=val}

function updateBgLabels(){

  bgOutput('bgOpacityO',bgNum('bgOpacity',100)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgBrightnessO',bgNum('bgBrightness',100)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgContrastO',bgNum('bgContrast',100)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgSaturationO',bgNum('bgSaturation',100)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgBlurO',bgNum('bgBlur',0)+'px');

  bgOutput('bgZoomO',bgNum('bgZoom',1).toFixed(2)+'×');

  bgOutput('bgXO',bgNum('bgX',50)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgYO',bgNum('bgY',50)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgRotO',bgNum('bgRot',0)+'°');

  bgOutput('bgHueO',bgNum('bgHue',190)+'°');

  bgOutput('bgPulseO',bgNum('bgPulse',25)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgAudioZoomO',bgNum('bgAudioZoom',12)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgMotionO',bgNum('bgMotion',18)+'%');

  bgOutput('bgAudioRotO',bgNum('bgAudioRot',8)+'°');

  bgOutput('bgSpeedO',bgNum('bgSpeed',1).toFixed(1)+'×');

}

updateBgLabels();

function getBgAudioLevel(){

  if(!fd||!fd.length) return 0;

  let bass=0,count=Math.min(90,fd.length);

  for(let i=0;i<count;i++) bass+=fd[i];

  bass/=Math.max(1,count*255);

  let energy=0;

  const step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(fd.length/96));

  for(let i=0;i<fd.length;i+=step) energy+=fd[i];

  energy/=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(fd.length/step)*255);

  const target=Math.min(1,bass*.72+energy*.28);

  bgEnergySmooth += (target-bgEnergySmooth)*.16;

  return bgEnergySmooth;

}

function resolveBgFit(v,i){

  if(v==='cover'||v==='contain'||v==='stretch') return {mode:v,x:0,y:0,zoom:1};

  const n=parseInt((v||'').replace('fit',''),10)||1;

  return {

    mode:['cover','contain','stretch'][n%3],

    x:((n*37)%101-50)/100,

    y:((n*53)%101-50)/100,

    zoom:0.75+((n*17)%126)/100

  };

}

function resolveBgBlend(v){

  if(v==='source-over'||v==='screen'||v==='lighter'||v==='overlay'||v==='soft-light'||v==='hard-light'||v==='multiply') return v;

  const n=parseInt((v||'').replace('blend',''),10)||1;

  const ops=["screen","lighter","overlay","soft-light","hard-light","multiply","darken","lighten",

             "color-dodge","color-burn","difference","exclusion","hue","saturation","color","luminosity"];

  return ops[n%ops.length];

}

function resolveBgEffect(v){

  if(["none","vignette","pulseGlow","rgb","colorize","mirror","kaleidoscope"].includes(v)) return v;

  const n=parseInt((v||'').replace('effect',''),10)||1;

  return ["vignette","pulseGlow","rgb","colorize","mirror","kaleidoscope"][n%6];

}

function drawBackground(w,h){

  if(!bgImage || !bg$('bgEnabled') || !bg$('bgEnabled').checked) return;

  const now=performance.now()/1000;

  const a=getBgAudioLevel();

  const speed=bgNum('bgSpeed',1);

  const pulse=bgNum('bgPulse',25)/100;

  const audioZoom=bgNum('bgAudioZoom',12)/100;

  const motion=bgNum('bgMotion',18)/100;

  const audioRot=bgNum('bgAudioRot',8);

  const baseZoom=bgNum('bgZoom',1);

  const baseX=bgNum('bgX',50)/100;

  const baseY=bgNum('bgY',50)/100;

  const z=baseZoom*(1+a*pulse*.55)+a*audioZoom*.12;

  const mx=Math.sin(now*speed*.72)*motion*a*0.16;

  const my=Math.cos(now*speed*.91)*motion*a*0.12;

  const rot=(bgNum('bgRot',0)+Math.sin(now*speed*.48)*audioRot*a)*Math.PI/180;

  const iw=bgImage.naturalWidth||bgImage.width;

  const ih=bgImage.naturalHeight||bgImage.height;

  if(!iw||!ih)return;

  let dw=w,dh=h;

  const fitCfg=resolveBgFit(bg$('bgFit')?.value||'cover');

  if(fitCfg.mode==='contain'){

    const sc=Math.min(w/iw,h/ih);

    dw=iw*sc;dh=ih*sc;

  }else if(fitCfg.mode==='cover'){

    const sc=Math.max(w/iw,h/ih);

    dw=iw*sc;dh=ih*sc;

  }

  dw*=z*fitCfg.zoom;dh*=z*fitCfg.zoom;

  const x=(w-dw)*(baseX+fitCfg.x) + w*mx;

  const y=(h-dh)*(baseY+fitCfg.y) + h*my;

  const opacity=bgNum('bgOpacity',100)/100;

  const brightness=bgNum('bgBrightness',100);

  const contrast=bgNum('bgContrast',100);

  const saturation=bgNum('bgSaturation',100);

  const blur=bgNum('bgBlur',0);

  const hueRotate=resolveBgEffect(bg$('bgEffect')?.value||'none')==='colorize' ? bgNum('bgHue',190) : 0;

  X.save();

  X.globalAlpha=opacity;

  X.globalCompositeOperation=resolveBgBlend(bg$('bgBlend')?.value||'source-over');

  X.filter=`brightness(${brightness}%) contrast(${contrast}%) saturate(${saturation}%) blur(${blur}px) hue-rotate(${hueRotate}deg)`;

  X.translate(w/2,h/2);

  X.rotate(rot);

  X.translate(-w/2,-h/2);

  const effect=resolveBgEffect(bg$('bgEffect')?.value||'none');

  if(effect==='mirror'){

    X.save();

    X.translate(w,0);X.scale(-1,1);

    X.drawImage(bgImage,x,y,dw,dh);

    X.restore();

    X.globalAlpha*=.45;

    X.drawImage(bgImage,x,y,dw,dh);

  }else if(effect==='rgb'){

    X.globalCompositeOperation='screen';

    X.globalAlpha*=.34;

    const shift=(2+a*12);

    X.filter=`brightness(${brightness}%) contrast(${contrast}%) saturate(${saturation}%) hue-rotate(${hueRotate}deg)`;

    X.drawImage(bgImage,x-shift,y,dw,dh);

    X.globalAlpha*=.72;

    X.drawImage(bgImage,x+shift,y,dw,dh);

    X.globalAlpha*=1.4;

    X.drawImage(bgImage,x,y,dw,dh);

  }else if(effect==='kaleidoscope'){

    const halfW=w/2,halfH=h/2;

    X.save();

    X.beginPath();X.rect(0,0,halfW,halfH);X.clip();

    X.drawImage(bgImage,x,y,dw,dh);X.restore();

    X.save();X.beginPath();X.rect(halfW,0,halfW,halfH);X.clip();

    X.translate(w,0);X.scale(-1,1);X.drawImage(bgImage,x,y,dw,dh);X.restore();

    X.save();X.beginPath();X.rect(0,halfH,halfW,halfH);X.clip();

    X.translate(0,h);X.scale(1,-1);X.drawImage(bgImage,x,y,dw,dh);X.restore();

    X.save();X.beginPath();X.rect(halfW,halfH,halfW,halfH);X.clip();

    X.translate(w,h);X.scale(-1,-1);X.drawImage(bgImage,x,y,dw,dh);X.restore();

  }else{

    X.drawImage(bgImage,x,y,dw,dh);

  }

  X.restore();

  if(effect==='vignette'||effect==='pulseGlow'){

    X.save();

    const alpha=effect==='pulseGlow' ? (.12+a*.26) : .55;

    const g=X.createRadialGradient(w/2,h/2,Math.min(w,h)*.12,w/2,h/2,Math.max(w,h)*.72);

    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');

    g.addColorStop(.62,`rgba(0,0,0,${alpha*.35})`);

    g.addColorStop(1,`rgba(0,0,0,${alpha})`);

    X.fillStyle=g;X.fillRect(0,0,w,h);

    X.restore();

  }

  if(effect==='pulseGlow' && a>.01){

    X.save();

    X.globalCompositeOperation='screen';

    X.globalAlpha=.10+a*.16;

    X.shadowBlur=25+a*55;

    X.shadowColor=`hsl(${hue},100%,60%)`;

    X.strokeStyle=`hsla(${hue},100%,60%,${.35+a*.35})`;

    X.lineWidth=2+a*8;

    X.strokeRect(4,4,w-8,h-8);

    X.restore();

  }

}

const bgFileEl=bg$('bgFile');

if(bgFileEl)bgFileEl.addEventListener('change',()=>{

  const file=bgFileEl.files&&bgFileEl.files[0];

  if(!file)return;

  if(bgImageURL)URL.revokeObjectURL(bgImageURL);

  bgImageURL=URL.createObjectURL(file);

  const im=new Image();

  im.onload=()=>{bgImage=im;updateBgLabels();draw();};

  im.src=bgImageURL;

});

['bgEnabled','bgFit','bgBlend','bgOpacity','bgBrightness','bgContrast','bgSaturation','bgBlur','bgZoom','bgX','bgY','bgRot','bgEffect','bgHue','bgPulse','bgAudioZoom','bgMotion','bgAudioRot','bgSpeed']

.forEach(id=>{

  const el=bg$(id);

  if(!el)return;

  el.addEventListener('input',()=>{updateBgLabels();});

  el.addEventListener('change',()=>{updateBgLabels();draw();});

});

bg$('bgRemove')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{

  if(bgImageURL)URL.revokeObjectURL(bgImageURL);

  bgImageURL='';bgImage=null;

  if(bgFileEl)bgFileEl.value='';

  draw();

});

bg$('bgReset')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{

  const defaults={

    bgEnabled:true,bgFit:'cover',bgBlend:'source-over',bgOpacity:100,bgBrightness:100,

    bgContrast:100,bgSaturation:100,bgBlur:0,bgZoom:1,bgX:50,bgY:50,bgRot:0,

    bgEffect:'none',bgHue:190,bgPulse:25,bgAudioZoom:12,bgMotion:18,bgAudioRot:8,bgSpeed:1

  };

  Object.entries(defaults).forEach(([id,v])=>{const el=bg$(id);if(el)el.value=v;});

  bg$('bgEnabled').checked=true;

  bgEnergySmooth=0;

  updateBgLabels();draw();

});

// BACKGROUND PARTICLE STUDIO

const particleState={

  enabled:true,preset:0,amount:280,size:1.8,speed:.85,opacity:72,spread:100,gravity:0,turbulence:18,

  audio:65,beat:35,trail:6,depth:48,color1:'#00e5ff',color2:'#7c4dff',shape:'circle',blend:'screen',direction:'orbit',twinkle:28,seed:1337

};

let particleItems=[];

let particleLastT=performance.now();

let particleRenderLast=0;

let particleLayer=null,particleLayerCtx=null,particleLayerW=0,particleLayerH=0;

function ensureParticleLayer(w,h){

  if(!particleLayer){particleLayer=document.createElement('canvas');particleLayerCtx=particleLayer.getContext('2d',{alpha:true});}

  if(particleLayerW!==w||particleLayerH!==h){particleLayerW=w;particleLayerH=h;particleLayer.width=w;particleLayer.height=h;particleLayerCtx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);particleRenderLast=0;}

}

const particleFamilies=[

 'Cosmic Dust','Neon Sparks','Galaxy Drift','Aurora Mist','Quantum Snow','Cyber Rain','Solar Flare','Electric Bloom','Crystal Shards','Plasma Embers',

 'Stellar Field','Magic Swirl','Hologram Dust','Meteor Stream','Ocean Biolights','Violet Comets','Fire Ash','Ice Crystals','Photon Trails','Dream Bubbles',

 'Gravity Seeds','Laser Fragments','Toxic Spores','Moonlight Specks','Rainbow Pixels','Void Particles','Diamond Storm','Energy Cells','Pulse Orbs','Spectral Dust'

];

const particleVariants=['Micro','Soft','Dense','Wild','Slow','Fast','Deep','Bright','Turbo','Ultra'];

const particleShapes=['circle','square','diamond','star','ring','cross','line','hex'];

function particleHash(n){let x=(n*1664525+1013904223)>>>0;x^=x>>>16;x=Math.imul(x,2246822507)>>>0;x^=x>>>13;return x>>>0}

function particleRand(i){return (particleHash(i+particleState.seed)%100000)/100000}

function buildParticlePresetList(){

  const el=$('particlePreset');

  if(!el)return;

  el.innerHTML='';

  for(let i=0;i<300;i++){

    const family=particleFamilies[Math.floor(i/10)];

    const variant=particleVariants[i%10];

    const name=String(i+1).padStart(3,'0')+' — '+family+' • '+variant;

    const o=document.createElement('option');o.value='p'+i;o.textContent=name;el.appendChild(o);

  }

  el.value='p0';

}

function particleApplyPreset(index){

  index=Math.max(0,Math.min(299,index|0));

  particleState.preset=index;

  const tier=Math.floor(index/30);

  const v=index%30;

  const seed=particleHash(index+71);

  particleState.seed=seed;

  particleState.amount=Math.round(140+(index%12)*22+Math.floor(index/30)*8);

  particleState.size=+(0.7+(v%8)*0.16+(tier%3)*0.12).toFixed(1);

  particleState.speed=+(0.22+(v%8)*0.10+(tier%5)*0.05).toFixed(2);

  particleState.opacity=Math.min(92,48+(v%8)*4+(tier%4)*3);

  particleState.spread=55+((v*11+index)%46);

  particleState.gravity=+((((index%7)-3)*0.05)).toFixed(2);

  particleState.turbulence=6+((v*5+index)%35);

  particleState.audio=30+((index*7)%71);

  particleState.beat=8+((index*11)%48);

  particleState.trail=(index*3)%9;

  particleState.depth=25+((index*13)%36);

  particleState.twinkle=8+((index*17)%38);

  particleState.shape=particleShapes[index%particleShapes.length];

  particleState.direction=['orbit','up','down','left','right','radial','random'][index%7];

  particleState.blend=['screen','lighter','source-over','overlay','soft-light','hard-light'][index%6];

  const c1=['#00e5ff','#7c4dff','#00ff9d','#ff3cac','#ffd166','#ff5f6d','#4facfe','#a8ff78'][index%8];

  const c2=['#7c4dff','#ff3cac','#00e5ff','#ffd166','#ff7b00','#7f7fd5','#00c6ff','#f093fb'][(index+3)%8];

  particleState.color1=c1;particleState.color2=c2;

  syncParticleControls();

  resetParticleItems();

}

function syncParticleControls(){

  const vals={particleAmount:particleState.amount,particleSize:particleState.size,particleSpeed:particleState.speed,particleOpacity:particleState.opacity,particleSpread:particleState.spread,particleGravity:particleState.gravity,particleTurbulence:particleState.turbulence,particleAudio:particleState.audio,particleBeat:particleState.beat,particleTrail:particleState.trail,particleDepth:particleState.depth,particleColor1:particleState.color1,particleColor2:particleState.color2,particleShape:particleState.shape,particleBlend:particleState.blend,particleDirection:particleState.direction,particleTwinkle:particleState.twinkle};

  Object.entries(vals).forEach(([id,v])=>{const e=$(id);if(e)e.value=v;});

  const outs={particleAmountO:Math.round(particleState.amount),particleSizeO:particleState.size.toFixed(1)+'px',particleSpeedO:particleState.speed.toFixed(1)+'×',particleOpacityO:Math.round(particleState.opacity)+'%',particleSpreadO:Math.round(particleState.spread)+'%',particleGravityO:particleState.gravity.toFixed(2),particleTurbulenceO:Math.round(particleState.turbulence)+'%',particleAudioO:Math.round(particleState.audio)+'%',particleBeatO:Math.round(particleState.beat)+'%',particleTrailO:Math.round(particleState.trail)+'%',particleDepthO:Math.round(particleState.depth)+'%',particleTwinkleO:Math.round(particleState.twinkle)+'%'};

  Object.entries(outs).forEach(([id,v])=>{const e=$(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;});

}

function resetParticleItems(){

  const w=C.width||1200,h=C.height||600;

  const n=Math.max(1,Math.min(420,Math.round(particleState.amount)));

  particleLastT=performance.now();

  particleItems=new Array(n);

  const spread=Math.max(.1,particleState.spread/100);

  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){

    const r=particleRand(i),r2=particleRand(i+9001),r3=particleRand(i+18002),r4=particleRand(i+27003);

    const edge=60;

    const x=edge+(w-edge*2)*r;

    const y=edge+(h-edge*2)*r2;

    const a=r3*Math.PI*2;

    const base=.18+.55*r4;

    const dirs=['orbit','radial','up','down','left','right','wander','spiral'];

    const localDir=dirs[(i+Math.floor(particleState.preset*1.7))%dirs.length];

    const speedMul=.55+particleRand(i+44000)*1.05;

    particleItems[i]={

      x,y,

      vx:Math.cos(a)*base*spread*speedMul,

      vy:Math.sin(a)*base*spread*speedMul,

      z:.18+r4*.82,

      phase:r*Math.PI*2,

      life:r2,

      age:particleRand(i+51000)*7,

      maxAge:5+particleRand(i+52000)*9,

      spin:(r3-.5)*2,

      sizeSeed:.65+r4*.7,

      dir:localDir,

      speedMul,

      wobble:.35+particleRand(i+53000)*1.2,

      orbitSign:particleRand(i+54000)>.5?1:-1

    };

  }

}

function particleAudioLevel(){

  if(!fd||!fd.length)return 0;

  let s=0,n=Math.min(96,fd.length);

  for(let i=0;i<n;i++)s+=fd[i];

  return Math.min(1,s/(n*255));

}

function particleBassLevel(){

  if(!fd||!fd.length)return 0;

  let n=Math.min(42,fd.length),s=0;

  for(let i=0;i<n;i++)s+=fd[i];

  return Math.min(1,s/(n*255));

}

function hexToRgb(hex){const m=/^#?([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})([a-f\d]{2})$/i.exec(hex||'');return m?{r:parseInt(m[1],16),g:parseInt(m[2],16),b:parseInt(m[3],16)}:{r:0,g:229,b:255};}

function mixParticleColor(a,b,t){const A=hexToRgb(a),B=hexToRgb(b),r=Math.round(A.r+(B.r-A.r)*t),g=Math.round(A.g+(B.g-A.g)*t),bb=Math.round(A.b+(B.b-A.b)*t);return `rgb(${r},${g},${bb})`;}

function drawParticleShape(ctx,x,y,size,shape,color,alpha,rot){

  ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(rot);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.7,size*.28);

  if(shape==='square'){ctx.fillRect(-size,-size,size*2,size*2);}

  else if(shape==='diamond'){ctx.rotate(Math.PI/4);ctx.fillRect(-size*.72,-size*.72,size*1.44,size*1.44);}

  else if(shape==='star'){ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/5,r=i%2?size*.42:size;ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r)}ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}

  else if(shape==='ring'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,size*.75,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();}

  else if(shape==='cross'){ctx.fillRect(-size*.16,-size,size*.32,size*2);ctx.fillRect(-size,-size*.16,size*2,size*.32);}

  else if(shape==='line'){ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,size*.45);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-size*2,0);ctx.lineTo(size*2,0);ctx.stroke();}

  else if(shape==='hex'){ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/3;ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*size,Math.sin(a)*size)}ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}

  else{ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,size,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}

  ctx.restore();

}

function drawBackgroundParticles(w,h){

  const en=$('particleEnabled');

  if(!en||!en.checked)return;

  ensureParticleLayer(w,h);

  const now=performance.now();

  if(now-particleRenderLast<33){

    X.drawImage(particleLayer,0,0,w,h);

    return;

  }

  particleRenderLast=now;

  if(!particleItems.length)resetParticleItems();

  const dt=Math.min(.033,Math.max(.001,(now-particleLastT)/1000));

  particleLastT=now;

  const energy=particleAudioLevel();

  const bass=particleBassLevel();

  const audioBoost=1+energy*(particleState.audio/65);

  const beatKick=(bass>.55)?(1+particleState.beat/90):1;

  const motion=particleState.speed*dt*60*audioBoost*beatKick;

  const spread=Math.max(.1,particleState.spread/100);

  const turb=particleState.turbulence/100;

  const gravity=particleState.gravity*motion;

  const cx=w/2,cy=h/2;

  const shape=particleState.shape,dir=particleState.direction;

  const audioWave=Math.sin(now*.006+energy*8)*energy;

  const L=particleLayerCtx;

  L.clearRect(0,0,w,h);

  L.globalCompositeOperation=particleState.blend;

  L.globalAlpha=1;

  for(let i=0;i<particleItems.length;i++){

    const p=particleItems[i];

    const dx=p.x-cx,dy=p.y-cy;

    const dist=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));

    const nx=dx/dist,ny=dy/dist;

    const pdir=p.dir||dir;

    const pmotion=motion*p.speedMul;

    if(pdir==='orbit'){

      const orbit=.018*pmotion*(1+p.z*1.2)*p.orbitSign;

      p.vx += -ny*orbit; p.vy += nx*orbit;

    }else if(pdir==='radial'){

      const push=.028*pmotion*(.55+energy*1.8)*p.z;

      p.vx += nx*push; p.vy += ny*push;

    }else if(pdir==='up') p.vy-=.024*pmotion*p.z;

    else if(pdir==='down') p.vy+=.024*pmotion*p.z;

    else if(pdir==='left') p.vx-=.024*pmotion*p.z;

    else if(pdir==='right') p.vx+=.024*pmotion*p.z;

    else if(pdir==='spiral'){

      const swirl=.012*pmotion*(1+p.z);

      p.vx += (-ny*.8+nx*.18)*swirl*p.orbitSign;

      p.vy += (nx*.8+ny*.18)*swirl*p.orbitSign;

    }else{

      const t=now*.001;

      p.vx += Math.cos(t*p.wobble+p.phase)*.010*pmotion*(1+turb*1.25);

      p.vy += Math.sin(t*p.wobble*1.13+p.phase*1.7)*.010*pmotion*(1+turb*1.25);

    }

    if(dir==='radial'){const push=.012*motion*(.4+energy)*p.z;p.vx+=nx*push;p.vy+=ny*push;}

    if(dir==='orbit'){const orbit=.006*motion*p.orbitSign;p.vx+=-ny*orbit;p.vy+=nx*orbit;}

    const beatPush=(bass>.62?0.028:0)*pmotion*p.z;

    p.vx += nx*beatPush; p.vy += ny*beatPush;

    p.vx += Math.sin(now*.0011+p.phase*2.1)*turb*.006*pmotion;

    p.vy += Math.cos(now*.0013+p.phase*1.3)*turb*.006*pmotion;

    p.vy += gravity*.010;

    p.vx*=.991;p.vy*=.991;

    const maxV=.62+particleState.speed*.34+p.z*.18;

    const v=Math.hypot(p.vx,p.vy);

    if(v>maxV){p.vx=p.vx/v*maxV;p.vy=p.vy/v*maxV;}

    p.x+=p.vx*pmotion*2.25;

    p.y+=p.vy*pmotion*2.25;

    p.age += dt;

    const margin=70;

    if(p.age>p.maxAge || p.x<-margin || p.x>w+margin || p.y<-margin || p.y>h+margin){

      const edgeRand=particleRand(i+Math.floor(now/1000)+p.life*1000);

      const side=Math.floor(edgeRand*4);

      if(side===0){p.x=-20;p.y=h*particleRand(i+7000);}

      else if(side===1){p.x=w+20;p.y=h*particleRand(i+8000);}

      else if(side===2){p.x=w*particleRand(i+9000);p.y=-20;}

      else {p.x=w*particleRand(i+10000);p.y=h+20;}

      const aa=particleRand(i+11000)*Math.PI*2;

      const s=.18+.5*particleRand(i+12000);

      p.vx=Math.cos(aa)*s*spread; p.vy=Math.sin(aa)*s*spread;

      p.age=0; p.maxAge=5+particleRand(i+13000)*9;

    }

    const tw=1-(particleState.twinkle/100)*.35+Math.sin(now*.004*(1+p.z*1.5)+p.phase)*(particleState.twinkle/100)*.35;

    const depth=.55+p.z*.55*(particleState.depth/100);

    const beatSize=1+(bass>.55?particleState.beat/240:0);

    const size=particleState.size*p.z*p.sizeSeed*beatSize*(.86+energy*particleState.audio/180);

    const alpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(particleState.opacity/100)*tw*depth));

    const color=mixParticleColor(particleState.color1,particleState.color2,(i/(particleItems.length||1)+p.z*.35)%1);

    if(particleState.trail>0 && size>.9){

      L.globalAlpha=alpha*(particleState.trail/100)*.25;

      L.strokeStyle=color;

      L.lineWidth=Math.max(.5,size*.24);

      L.beginPath();L.moveTo(p.x-p.vx*5,p.y-p.vy*5);L.lineTo(p.x,p.y);L.stroke();

    }

    drawParticleShape(L,p.x,p.y,size,shape,color,alpha,p.phase+p.spin*now*.001+audioWave*.05);

  }

  L.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';

  L.globalAlpha=1;

  X.drawImage(particleLayer,0,0,w,h);

}

buildParticlePresetList();

particleApplyPreset(0);

$('particlePreset')?.addEventListener('change',e=>particleApplyPreset(parseInt((e.target.value||'p0').slice(1),10)||0));

$('particleEnabled')?.addEventListener('change',()=>{});

['particleAmount','particleSize','particleSpeed','particleOpacity','particleSpread','particleGravity','particleTurbulence','particleAudio','particleBeat','particleTrail','particleDepth','particleTwinkle'].forEach(id=>{

  $(id)?.addEventListener('input',()=>{

    particleState.amount=+$('particleAmount').value;particleState.size=+$('particleSize').value;particleState.speed=+$('particleSpeed').value;particleState.opacity=+$('particleOpacity').value;particleState.spread=+$('particleSpread').value;particleState.gravity=+$('particleGravity').value;particleState.turbulence=+$('particleTurbulence').value;particleState.audio=+$('particleAudio').value;particleState.beat=+$('particleBeat').value;particleState.trail=+$('particleTrail').value;particleState.depth=+$('particleDepth').value;particleState.twinkle=+$('particleTwinkle').value;

    syncParticleControls();

    if(id==='particleAmount')resetParticleItems();

  });

});

['particleColor1','particleColor2','particleShape','particleBlend','particleDirection'].forEach(id=>$(id)?.addEventListener('change',()=>{particleState[id.replace('particle','').replace(/^./,c=>c.toLowerCase())]=$(id).value;}));

$('particleRandom')?.addEventListener('click',()=>particleApplyPreset(Math.floor(Math.random()*300)));

$('particleReset')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{particleApplyPreset(0);$('particleEnabled').checked=true;});

</script>

</body>

</html>
